Anak's Creation!
Map
Park + HighWays + FlyOvers + Island
Hello i made this map when i was free.. first i was going to release it as FS by adding some functions..,gates... etc..
i map there is an highway on South end of strip in LV .. this highway later turn into flyover and land into an park with flowers .. fountains ... trees different types... bushes... and some deers ( lol two deers fucking)...
then from that park another flyover starts which lands into an island / beach .. there is an dance floor ... trees... shops... seats... parkings... an volcano... and much more you can see in pictures...
